3185: // =============================================================================
3186: // POST /api/documents/:id/sign-wallet — KS-278 (KS-274 decision)
3187: // =============================================================================
3188: //
3189: // Dedicated endpoint for a DETACHED Cardano wallet signature, mirroring
3190: // /sign-cert. The caller signs the source document's contentHash with their
3191: // wallet (CIP-30 signData) and submits {walletAddress, signature, key}. The
3192: // signature is out-of-band — the bytes don't change — so this mints a new
3193: // lineage row whose contentHash EQUALS the source's, carrying the wallet
3194: // signature envelope in data.sigWallet.
3195: //
3196: // Per KS-274, K verifies the signature SERVER-SIDE (Ed25519 over the COSE
3197: // Sig_structure1, via @secuura/shared verifyMessageSignature) against the
3198: // source's contentHash BEFORE minting — a bogus signature is rejected, never
3199: // recorded or anchored. Anchoring is NOT auto-triggered (call .../anchor).
3200: //
3201: // Why a dedicated endpoint and not a /version action: a wallet signature is
3202: // detached (no new bytes), so it doesn't fit /version's newContentHash model —
3203: // same reasoning as /sign-cert.
3204: documentsRouter.post(
3205:   '/:id/sign-wallet',
3206:   [
3207:     param('id').notEmpty().withMessage('Document ID is required'),
3208:     body('walletAddress').isString().notEmpty().withMessage('walletAddress is required'),
3209:     body('signature').isString().notEmpty().withMessage('signature (hex COSESign1) is required'),
3210:     body('key').isString().notEmpty().withMessage('key (hex COSEKey) is required'),
3211:     body('metadata').optional().isObject().withMessage('metadata must be an object'),
3212:     body('title').optional().isString(),
3213:   ],
3214:   async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
3215:     try {
3216:       const user = getUserFromRequest(req);
3217:       if (!user) {
3218:         return res.status(401).json({ success: false, error: { code: 'UNAUTHORIZED', message: 'Authentication required' } });
3219:       }
3220: 
3221:       if (!isAllowedByRoleOrScope(req, ALLOWED_VERSION_ROLES, 'documents:write')) {
3222:         return res.status(403).json({
3223:           success: false,
3224:           error: { code: 'FORBIDDEN', message: 'Your role / scope does not permit signing this document' },
3225:         });
3226:       }
3227: 
3228:       const errors = validationResult(req);
3229:       if (!errors.isEmpty()) {
3230:         return res.status(400).json({
3231:           success: false,
3232:           error: { code: 'VALIDATION_ERROR', message: 'Invalid request body', details: errors.array() },
3233:         });
3234:       }
3235: 
3236:       const { id } = req.params;
3237:       const walletAddress = String(req.body.walletAddress).trim();
3238:       const signature = String(req.body.signature).trim();
3239:       const key = String(req.body.key).trim();
3240:       const metadata = (req.body?.metadata && typeof req.body.metadata === 'object')
3241:         ? (req.body.metadata as Record<string, unknown>)
3242:         : {};
3243:       const overrideTitle = typeof req.body?.title === 'string' ? req.body.title.trim() : '';
3244: 
3245:       // 1. Resolve source doc + tenant scope.
3246:       const tenantId = getReqTenantId(req);
3247:       const source = await getDocument(id, tenantId, (req as any).db);
3248:       if (!source) {
3249:         return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: { code: 'NOT_FOUND', message: 'Document not found in this tenant' } });
3250:       }
3251:       // KS-1213: stored as `source.type`, served as `data.documentType || type`; same refusal as /version.
3252:       if (metadata.documentType !== undefined && metadata.documentType !== source.type) {
3253:         return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: { code: 'BAD_REQUEST', message: 'metadata.documentType must equal the source document type' } });
3254:       }
3255: 
3256:       // 2. Verify the wallet signature SERVER-SIDE (KS-274): the wallet must
3257:       //    have signed THIS document's contentHash. Reject before minting.
3258:       const signatureValid = verifyMessageSignature(walletAddress, source.contentHash, signature, key);
3259:       if (!signatureValid) {
3260:         return res.status(400).json({
3261:           success: false,
3262:           error: {
3263:             code: 'INVALID_WALLET_SIGNATURE',
3264:             message: 'The wallet signature did not verify against this document\'s content hash. Ensure the wallet signed the document contentHash with the submitted address/key.',
3265:           },
3266:         });
3267:       }
3268: 
3269:       // 3. Mint the new versioned document row. contentHash matches the source
3270:       //    (bytes unchanged); the wallet signature lives in the data blob.
3271:       const newExternalId = `doc-${Date.now()}-${uuidv4().slice(0, 8)}`;
3272:       try {
3273:         await assertNoCycle(newExternalId, id, tenantId, { db: (req as any).db });
3274:       } catch (cycleErr: any) {
3275:         if (cycleErr?.code === 'LINEAGE_CYCLE') {
3276:           return res.status(422).json({
3277:             success: false,
3278:             error: {
3279:               code: 'LINEAGE_CYCLE',
3280:               message: `Refusing to sign: source ${id} would form a cycle in the lineage chain (corrupted parent chain?)`,
3281:             },
3282:           });
3283:         }
3284:         throw cycleErr;
3285:       }
3286: 
3287:       const now = new Date().toISOString();
3288:       const sourceTitle = (source.data?.title as string) || source.type || 'Untitled';
3289:       const derivedTitle = overrideTitle || `${sourceTitle} (wallet-signed)`;
3290: 
3291:       const sigWalletEnvelope = {
3292:         version: 1,
3293:         documentId: newExternalId,
3294:         parentDocumentId: id,
3295:         signedContentHash: source.contentHash,
3296:         signature,
3297:         key,
3298:         walletAddress,
3299:         signatureAlgorithm: 'CIP-8/Ed25519',
3300:         signedAt: now,
3301:         signedById: user.id,
3302:       };
3303: 
3304:       const derivedDoc: DocumentRecord = {
3305:         id: newExternalId,
3306:         type: source.type,
3307:         status: 'signed',
3308:         owner: source.owner,
3309:         rightsHolderId: source.rightsHolderId,
3310:         parentDocumentId: id,
3311:         data: {
3312:           ...source.data,
3313:           title: derivedTitle,
3314:           ...metadata,
3315:           // KS-1508: the server-owned keys come AFTER the caller's metadata (see /version).
3316:           versionAction: 'sign-wallet',
3317:           versionedAt: now,
3318:           versionedById: user.id,
3319:           parentDocumentId: id,
3320:           // Wallet-signature envelope — stored alongside the versioned doc.
3321:           sigWallet: sigWalletEnvelope,
3322:         },
3323:         contentHash: source.contentHash, // unchanged — detached wallet signature
3324:         signatures: [],
3325:         blockchain: undefined,
3326:         createdAt: now,
3327:         updatedAt: now,
3328:       };
3329: 
3330:       await saveDocument(derivedDoc, tenantId, (req as any).db);
3331: 
3332:       logger.info('Document signed via wallet', {
3333:         sourceId: id,
3334:         newId: newExternalId,
3335:         walletAddress: walletAddress.slice(0, 16),
3336:         tenantId,
3337:         userId: user.id,
3338:       });
3339: 
3340:       publishEvent('document.versioned' as never, {
3341:         sourceId: id,
3342:         newId: newExternalId,
3343:         action: 'sign-wallet',
3344:         contentHash: source.contentHash,
3345:         userId: user.id,
3346:       }).catch(() => {});
3347: 
3348:       return res.status(201).json({
3349:         document: {
3350:           id: newExternalId,
3351:           parentDocumentId: id,
3352:           type: source.type,
3353:           status: 'signed',
3354:           contentHash: source.contentHash,
3355:           action: 'sign-wallet',
3356:           title: derivedTitle,
3357:           versionedAt: now,
3358:           anchored: false,
3359:         },
3360:         sigWallet: sigWalletEnvelope,
3361:       });
3362:     } catch (error) {
3363:       logger.error('Failed to sign document via wallet', {
3364:         error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
3365:       });
3366:       return res.status(500).json({
3367:         success: false,
3368:         error: { code: 'INTERNAL_ERROR', message: 'Failed to wallet-sign document' },
3369:       });
3370:     }
3371:   },
3372: );
3373: 
3374: // =============================================================================
3375: // GET /api/documents/:id/sig-json — KS-83 PR 3 (public, see KS-87)
3376: // =============================================================================
3377: //
3378: // Returns the out-of-band signature envelope for a doc that was signed
3379: // via POST /sign-cert. Per KS-21 Q3 v1 decision: the envelope is a
3380: // separate companion artifact, NOT embedded in the file. Offline verifiers
3381: // download both the original bytes and this envelope.
3382: //
3383: // No auth on this endpoint — same as the public verifier. The envelope
3384: // only contains data that's already meant for offline distribution
3385: // (signature, public cert, content hash). Revoked-cert state is NOT
3386: // inferred here — the verifier checks current cert status against
3387: // /api/issuer-certs/{id} or via the published CRL (future).
3388: //
3389: // KS-87: registered on publicDocumentsRouter (not documentsRouter) so the
3390: // blanket `authenticate()` on documentsRouter doesn't fire. Mounted before
3391: // documentsRouter in index.ts so Express matches this specific path first.
3392: publicDocumentsRouter.get(
3393:   '/:id/sig-json',
3394:   [param('id').notEmpty().withMessage('Document ID is required')],
3395:   async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
3396:     try {
3397:       const { id } = req.params;
3398:       const tenantId = getReqTenantId(req);
3399:       const doc = await getDocument(id, tenantId, (req as any).db);
3400:       if (!doc) {
3401:         return res.status(404).json({
3402:           success: false,
3403:           error: { code: 'NOT_FOUND', message: 'Document not found' },
3404:         });
3405:       }
3406: 
3407:       const sigJson = (doc.data as Record<string, unknown>)?.sigJson;
3408:       if (!sigJson || typeof sigJson !== 'object') {
3409:         return res.status(404).json({
3410:           success: false,
3411:           error: {
3412:             code: 'NO_SIG_JSON',
3413:             message: 'This document does not have an X.509 signature envelope. Was it created via POST /api/documents/{id}/sign-cert?',
3414:           },
3415:         });
3416:       }
3417: 
3418:       // Content-Disposition so the response can be saved straight to disk
3419:       // as `<id>.sig.json` — convenient for SSD-side download flows.
3420:       res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/json');
3421:       res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', `inline; filename="${id}.sig.json"`);
3422:       return res.status(200).json(sigJson);
3423:     } catch (error) {
3424:       logger.error('Failed to get sig-json', {
3425:         error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
3426:       });
3427:       return res.status(500).json({
3428:         success: false,
3429:         error: { code: 'INTERNAL_ERROR', message: 'Failed to fetch sig-json' },
3430:       });
3431:     }
3432:   },
3433: );
3434: 
3435: export default documentsRouter;
